Abstract: Multimodal sarcasm detection aims to identify sarcastic intent from multimodal content, where inconsistencies between literal meaning and contextual cues often signal irony. This task has attracted increasing research attention. However, accurate detection remains challenging due to instance-dependent modality contributions and misleading semantic consistency, where surface-level alignment masks underlying contradictory intent. Existing methods often rely on fixed fusion strategies and treat sarcasm as generic cross-modal mismatch, limiting their ability to capture subtle sarcasm cues and instance-specific modality interactions. To address these challenges, we propose a novel MSD framework that integrates Dynamic Gated Cross-Modal Fusion with Sarcastic-aware Contrastive Regularization (SaCR). Specifically, a bidirectional gated interaction module performs cross-modal feature filtering and adaptively calibrates textual and visual contributions at the instance level. A dynamic fusion gate further balances modality importance to generate more robust multimodal representations. Furthermore, SaCR is introduced as a label-aware contrastive regularization objective that encourages semantic consistency for non-sarcastic samples while suppressing misleading consistency in sarcastic cases. The proposed framework is trained end-to-end with a multi-objective learning strategy that jointly optimizes multimodal classification and auxiliary unimodal supervision. Extensive experiments on MMSD and MMSD2.0 demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms strong baselines.

</details>

Abstract: Natural language code retrieval is a rapidly evolving task in computer science. However, the 1C:Enterprise ecosystem combines Russian syntax with highly domain-specific terminology, for which open datasets and specialized models have been virtually non-existent. We present a comprehensive pipeline for 1C code retrieval: an open benchmark of 3,413 real-world, PII-scrubbed query-code pairs, a reproducible evaluation harness, and a specialized bi-encoder. To overcome scarce labeled data, we fine-tune on 784,057 synthetic triplets generated by google/gemma-4-26B-A4B-it from public code repositories, using Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) and a privacy-aware tokenizer. Because the benchmark subsets differ in size, we report balanced-subset macro, query-weighted micro, and forum-only results. Our model reaches 0.5992 balanced macro nDCG@10, 0.5044 micro, and 0.4617 on forum, versus 0.4932 macro for the baseline architecture and 0.5404 for google/embeddinggemma-300m. Removing every benchmark example flagged by the conservative exact/13-gram overlap audit leaves 0.6011 balanced macro (0.5010 micro), indicating that detected train-benchmark overlap does not explain the headline result. MRL truncation to 256 dimensions preserves 99.9% of retrieval quality while reducing dense-index storage and exact similarity arithmetic by a factor of three.

</details>

*Zhifa Geng,Subin Huang,Hao Guo,Junjie Chen,Sanmin Liu,Chao Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种迭代式代理校正框架，用于解决不完整多模态情感分析中语言模态退化的问题。该方法从非语言模态构建语言代理，并通过门控残差校正逐步精炼，再根据语言可靠性得分自适应融合，显著提升缺失模态下的情感预测鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的多模态输入往往不完整或被破坏，这削弱了跨模态互补性并引入误导信息。现有基于代理的方法通常采用一次性代理构建来补偿退化的语言信息，但初始生成的代理可能粗糙或不可靠，过早注入此类代理会传播初始错误并损害情感预测精度。

Method: 1) 从非语言模态构建面向语言的代理；2) 在多模态上下文中通过门控残差校正迭代精炼代理；3) 基于估计的语言可靠性得分，将校正后的代理与观察到的语言表示自适应融合；4) 引入阶段式潜在校正目标，利用完整语言表示作为训练时的语义锚点，稳定代理精炼轨迹。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis aims to infer affective states by integrating language, visual, and acoustic cues. However, real-world multimodal inputs are often incomplete or corrupted, which can weaken cross-modal complementarity and introduce misleading information into downstream fusion. Existing proxy-based methods for incomplete MSA commonly rely on one-shot proxy construction to compensate for degraded language information, but the generated proxy may be coarse or unreliable at initialization. Prematurely injecting such a proxy into multimodal reasoning can propagate initial errors and compromise sentiment prediction. To address this limitation, we propose an iterative proxy correction framework for robust incomplete MSA. Our method constructs a language-oriented proxy from non-language modalities and progressively refines it under multimodal context through gated residual correction. The corrected proxy is then adaptively fused with the observed language representation according to an estimated language reliability score, allowing the model to balance proxy-based compensation and trustworthy linguistic evidence. In addition, we introduce a stage-wise latent correction objective that uses the complete language representation as a training-time semantic anchor to stabilize the proxy refinement trajectory. Extensive experiments on MOSI, MOSEI, and SIMS under diverse missing-modality settings demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms competitive baselines and achieves robust sentiment prediction under incomplete inputs.

</details>

Jin,Xing Wu,Jinghui Lu,Abdul Samad,Akbar Faruqi,Cesar Caraballo,Cibele Brandão,Dhruva,Gupta,Eunji Jeon,Gabriel Madera-Santiago,Geon Lee,Hugo Toshio Itikawa,Insook Cho,Isabelli Martins,Isarar Siddique,Israr Ahmed,Jihyo Kwak,Kanyakorn Veerakanjana,Luis Guilherme Cardoso,Minjin Kim,Piyalitt Ittichaiwong,Renee Dua,Santiago Gudiño-Rosales,Xiujie Chen,Zeo Lapalus,Zixin Xu,Michihiro Yasunaga,Rex Ying,Heuiseok Lim,Jaewoo Kang,Chanjun Park,Hang Jiang,Ethan Goh,Hyunjae Kim,Edison Marrese-Taylor,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Qingyu Chen,Irene Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HealMed，一个由专家评审的医学多语言评测基准，包含9种语言各1000个样本，涵盖MCQA、NLI和开放式问答三种任务格式。研究发现低资源语言上的性能下降最明显，专有模型跨语言稳定性最好，而医学专业化本身并不能保证多语言鲁棒性，且专家修订对翻译质量有实质性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学大语言模型评测大多集中在高资源语言（尤其是英语），缺乏对多语言场景，特别是低资源语言的系统性评估。同时，翻译质量对跨语言评测结果的影响未被充分量化，需要由医学专家严格评审的多语言医学评测基准来填补这一空白。

Method: HealMed基准由23位来自9个国家和地区的医学专家历时两年构建，从9个数据集中精选，每个语言包含1000个样本，涵盖多选问答（MCQA）、自然语言推理（NLI）和开放式问答三种任务格式。每个翻译均由两位精通英语和目标语言的专家进行评审和修订。

Result: 低资源语言上的模型性能下降最为显著，但下降幅度因语言和模型而异。最强力的专有模型跨语言表现最稳定，而许多开源模型和医学专用模型表现出更大且不一致的性能差距。医学专门化并不能保证多语言鲁棒性。专家修订可能提高或降低测量性能，说明翻译质量会实质性影响跨语言评测结果。

Conclusion: HealMed作为专家评审的多语言医学评测基准，揭示了当前大语言模型在医学多语言场景下的性能差距问题，强调翻译质量对评测有效性的关键作用，并指出医学专门化不足以解决跨语言鲁棒性问题，为未来医学多语言模型的发展提供了评测基础和改进方向。

Abstract: We present HealMed, an expert-reviewed benchmark for multilingual evaluation of large language models in medicine. HealMed contains 1,000 examples in each of nine languages, drawn from nine datasets and covering three task formats: MCQA, NLI and open-ended QA. The benchmark was developed over two years by 23 physicians and medical experts based across nine countries and regions. Each translation was evaluated and revised by two experts fluent in English and the corresponding target language. On HealMed, performance declined most in low-resource languages, although the size of the gap varied markedly across languages and models. The strongest proprietary models were the most stable across languages, whereas many open-source and medically specialized models showed larger and less consistent gaps. Medical specialization alone did not ensure multilingual robustness. Furthermore, expert revision could either raise or lower measured performance, indicating that translation quality materially affects cross-language evaluation results.

</details>


### [41] [SABET-QA: Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20083)
*Brahim Touayouch,Mirette Moawad,Dmitry Akulov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SABET-QA框架，通过多跳迭代推理、双向实体-时间评分和槽感知上下文模块，在多个TKGQA基准上优于强基线，尤其在复杂多步时间查询上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序知识图谱问答(TKGQA)需要推理时间敏感事实，但现有基于嵌入的方法依赖单次前向推理管线，难以处理多步查询，缺乏迭代推理能力。

Method: 提出SABET-QA框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 多跳迭代推理机制，逐步细化推理状态；(2) 双向实体-时间评分机制，同时从实体和时间两个维度进行打分；(3) 槽感知上下文化模块，将问题语义与时序知识图谱嵌入对齐。此外，采用可微分工作记忆支持渐进式假设精化，并在可用时以辅助时间边界提供粗粒度监督。

Result: 在CronQuestions、Complex-CronQuestions、MultiTQ和TimeQuestions四个基准上均取得一致改进，尤其对复杂多步时序查询的提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 多跳迭代推理结合双向实体-时间评分与问题语义对齐，能有效解决TKGQA中嵌入方法在复杂多步查询上的推理瓶颈。

Abstract: Question Answering over Temporal Knowledge Graphs (TKGQA) requires reasoning over time-sensitive facts, yet existing embedding-based methods struggle with multi-step queries due to single-pass reasoning pipelines. We propose SABET-QA, a framework that iteratively refines reasoning states across multiple hops via a bidirectional entity-temporal scoring mechanism and a slot-aware contextualization module that aligns question semantics with temporal KG embeddings. A differentiable working memory enables progressive hypothesis refinement, while auxiliary temporal boundaries serve as coarse supervision when available. Experiments on CronQuestions, Complex-CronQuestions, MultiTQ, and TimeQuestions demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, particularly on complex multi-step temporal queries.

</details>


### [42] [OenoBench: A Wine-Domain Benchmark for Knowledge-Grounded Evaluation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20106)
*Nikita Khudov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出OenoBench，一个包含3,266道葡萄酒领域多项选择题的基准测试集，涵盖六大支柱和四个难度层级，基于38,104条溯源事实构建。通过LLM驱动的构建管线（LLM仅负责格式化与审核，事实来源为URL），评估了16种前沿模型配置，发现准确率在53%-84%之间，o3领先（83.6%），并揭示了推理模式提升差异、模型自偏好、开源与专有模型成本-准确率Pareto前沿等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 葡萄酒领域缺乏结构化的专业知识基准，现有基准往往依赖LLM生成内容作为事实来源，无法保证知识可靠性。作者旨在构建一个每个声明都锚定到可验证URL的可信领域基准，以公平、可审计地评估前沿模型在该垂直领域的能力。

Method: 从35个经过来源验证的爬虫中收集政府注册机构（INAO、TTB、OIV）、同行评审期刊及Wikipedia/Wikidata的38,104条原子事实；采用LLM驱动管线（LLM仅负责重排和审计而非事实来源）；五种生成策略与五个生成器家族生成问题；九代理审计系统对照人类金标准（Cohen's κ校准）评分。

Result: 16种前沿配置的评估显示：①准确率跨度53%-84%，o3以83.6%领先；②推理模式提升集中在DeepSeek R1（+6.8pp），在Claude Opus和Gemini Pro中无提升；③Anthropic自家问题自偏好+9pp，Google则反向偏好-8pp；④开放权重模型与专有推理模型共享成本-准确率Pareto前沿；⑤各配置在闭卷可解项上提升约33pp，揭示仅情境切片能避免的参数性回忆天花板。

Conclusion: OenoBench作为首个完全溯源的葡萄酒领域基准，证明了去中心化事实来源构建领域基准的可行性，同时系统性地揭示了当前前沿模型在垂直领域知识评估中的推理模式增益不均、供应商自偏好偏差及参数性回忆天花板等普遍性问题。全部资源以CC-BY-SA-4.0许可证发布。

Abstract: We introduce OenoBench, a wine-domain knowledge benchmark of 3,266 multiple-choice questions across six pillars (regions, grape varieties, viticulture, winemaking, producers, business) and four difficulty tiers. The corpus is built from 38,104 atomic, source-anchored facts extracted by 35 provenance-verified scrapers from government registries (INAO, TTB, OIV), peer-reviewed journals, and Wikipedia/Wikidata. Our methodological contribution is an LLM-driven pipeline in which language models reformat verified facts and audit the result, but never serve as the source of truth: every claim traces to a URL, every question is generated by one of five strategies across five generator families, and every question is scored by a nine-agent audit calibrated against a human gold sheet via Cohen's $κ$. Evaluating sixteen frontier configurations, we find: (i) overall accuracy spans 53%-84%, led by o3 at 83.6%; (ii) reasoning-mode lift concentrates in DeepSeek R1 (+6.8pp) and is absent in Claude Opus and Gemini Pro; (iii) Anthropic shows +9pp self preference on its own questions while Google shows -8pp inverse preference; (iv) frontier open-weight models share the cost-vs-accuracy Pareto frontier with proprietary reasoning models; and (v) every config gains around 33pp on closed-book solvable items, revealing a parametric-recall ceiling that only the contextual slice avoids. We release corpus, audit findings, human-review app, and construction code under CC-BY-SA-4.0.

</details>


### [43] [When Text and Numbers Disagree: Evidence Arbitration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20116)
*Mattia Carletti,Edward Phillips,Fredrik K. Gustafsson,Patitapaban Palo,Lei Clifton,Danielle Belgrave,Xiao Gu,David A. Clifton*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过受控合成基准测试，系统探究了LLM在多源证据冲突时（如文本摘要与数值数据、外部工具输出与上下文证据）的仲裁行为，发现模型倾向于采用启发式策略（如偏好文本或数字、重视时间新近性而非可靠性提示），而非基于证据可靠性的理性权衡，暴露了工具增强决策系统的潜在失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地应用于需要整合文本摘要、数值观测和外部工具输出等异构证据的场景，而这些来源常提供相互冲突的信息。然而，现有研究对LLM如何在冲突证据之间进行仲裁缺乏系统性的理解，尤其是其仲裁行为是否遵循理性原则还是依赖启发式策略。

Method: 作者设计了一个受控合成基准：通过潜在风险轨迹同时生成数值时间序列和自然语言摘要，构造"恰好只有一个证据来源与真实标签一致"的冲突场景。该设计允许独立操控模态（文本vs数值）、时间新近性、来源可靠性和证据来源等变量，并在多个开源指令微调模型上进行系统实验。

Result: 实验发现：1) 模型的仲裁行为具有系统性而非随机性；2) 模型表现出明显的"文本vs数值"偏好差异；3) 模型对时间新近性的遵循程度高于对显式可靠性线索的遵循；4) 模型可能过度依赖外部预测，即使这些预测与直接上下文证据冲突。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在整合异构证据时往往依赖启发式仲裁策略而非基于证据实际可靠性的理性决策，这揭示了工具增强决策系统中的一个重要失败模式，提示需要改进模型在冲突证据下的推理与权衡能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in settings where textual summaries, numerical observations, and external tool outputs may provide conflicting evidence. We study how LLMs arbitrate between such sources when they support opposing decisions. To do so, we introduce a controlled synthetic benchmark in which latent risk trajectories generate both numerical time series and natural language summaries, allowing us to construct conflicts where exactly one evidence source is aligned with the ground-truth label. This design lets us independently manipulate modality, temporal recency, source reliability, and evidence provenance. Across open-weight instruction-tuned models, we find that arbitration behaviour is systematic rather than random: models exhibit distinct text-versus-number preferences, follow temporal recency more consistently than explicit reliability cues, and can over-rely on external forecasts even when they conflict with direct contextual evidence. These results suggest that current LLMs often rely on heuristic arbitration strategies when integrating heterogeneous evidence, highlighting a failure mode for tool-augmented decision systems.

</details>


### [44] [FormalTCS: Benchmarking End-to-End Frontier Formal Theoretical Computer Science Research of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20153)
*Dingzirui Wang,Xuanliang Zhang,Keyan Xu,Qingfu Zhu,Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个专家验证的基准测试，用于评估LLM在前沿、端到端的理论计算机科学（TCS）研究中的能力，包含来自2025-2026年STOC、FOCS、SODA和COLT会议的175个实例，并附带专家验证的Lean形式化证明。评估显示当前模型在自动化TCS研究方面仍有很大差距，自动形式化是最突出的瓶颈，且模型还缺乏"研究品味"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在自动化理论计算机科学研究方面展现出日益增长的潜力，但现有基准测试远不能反映真实的研究场景，缺乏能评估LLM完成端到端前沿TCS研究能力的专家验证基准。

Method: 构建了一个包含175个实例的基准测试，这些实例取自2025-2026年STOC、FOCS、SODA和COLT会议论文，保留了论文特有的定义、假设和证明依赖关系，并附有专家验证的Lean形式化定义及证明。同时基于该基准开发了一个自动化TCS研究框架，能够生成、形式化、筛选和证明新主张。

Result: 最佳模型在将自然语言主张翻译为形式化定理陈述时仅达到11.5分（自动形式化），而在证明人类提供的正式陈述时达到28.6 Pass@8。在生成的64个主张中，仅有6个最终通过了专家评估和证明验证。

Conclusion: 当前LLM远未达到可靠完成完整TCS研究流程的能力，自动形式化是最尖锐的瓶颈；此外，即便解决了形式化问题，模型在研究品味（即筛选有价值主张的能力）上的局限是自主TCS研究的另一大障碍。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown growing potential for automated theoretical computer science (TCS) research, yet existing benchmarks remain far from realistic research settings. We introduce \ourbenchmark, an expert-validated benchmark for evaluating LLMs on frontier, end-to-end TCS research. \ourbenchmark contains $175$ instances drawn from papers accepted to STOC, FOCS, SODA, and COLT in 2025-2026, preserving paper-specific definitions, assumptions, and proof dependencies, with expert-verified Lean formalizations and proofs. Evaluations of leading LLMs reveal that current models remain far from reliably completing the full research pipeline. In particular, autoformalization is the sharpest bottleneck: the best model achieves only $11.5$ on translating natural-language claims into formal theorem statements, compared with $28.6$ Pass@8 when proving human-provided formal statements. Building on \ourbenchmark, we further develop an automated TCS research framework that generates, formalizes, filters, and proves new claims. Of $64$ generated claims, only $6$ ultimately pass expert evaluation and proof verification, indicating that beyond formalization, limited research taste remains another major barrier to autonomous TCS research.

</details>


### [45] [Task-CoEvolve: Efficient Harness Optimization via Adaptive Validation Task Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20169)
*Atsuyuki Miyai,Kiyoharu Aizawa,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Task-CoEvolve方法，通过自适应验证任务选择来高效优化LLM智能体harness。该方法利用候选harness间对任务的"分歧度"来判断信息量，采用方差加权采样聚焦于智能体能力边界附近的任务，同时通过采样概率校准来估计全量验证集性能。在在线文本分类和Terminal-Bench 2.1上，Task-CoEvolve匹配了全量搜索的最终性能，同时将优化过程中的评估次数减少80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体harness优化需要通过迭代重写harness代码来提升验证性能，但现有方法在每次迭代都对固定验证集进行全量评估，导致即使某些任务在harness演化后已丧失判别力，仍被重复评估，造成大量不必要的计算开销。

Method: Task-CoEvolve通过两个关键机制解决上述问题：(1) 信息量任务选择——基于"候选harness在哪些任务上分歧越大越具信息量"的观察，利用历史结果的方差加权采样，将评估集中在智能体能力边界附近的任务上，采样分布随harness演化自适应调整；(2) 全量性能估计——根据采样概率对部分评估结果进行加权校准，使得在不同迭代中评估不同子集时仍能进行一致的性能比较。该方法实现了验证任务与harness的共同演化。

Result: 在在线文本分类和Terminal-Bench 2.1基准上，Task-CoEvolve一致优于固定子集基线，匹配了全量集搜索的最终性能，同时将优化期间的评估次数减少了80%。

Conclusion: 通过验证任务与harness协同演化，可以在不牺牲最终性能的前提下大幅降低harness优化过程中的评估成本，为高效智能体harness优化提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to efficient LLM agent harness optimization through adaptive validation task selection. Harness optimization iteratively rewrites the harness code based on validation performance, enabling substantial performance gains without updating the underlying model weights. Existing approaches, however, evaluate a fixed validation set in full at every iteration, incurring substantial evaluation costs even on tasks that become less discriminative as the harness evolves. We propose $\textbf{Task-CoEvolve}$, which co-evolves the validation tasks with the harness by addressing two challenges: selecting informative tasks and estimating full-set performance from partial evaluations. Task-CoEvolve builds on the observation that tasks on which candidate harnesses disagree are more informative for distinguishing among them than tasks that are consistently solved or failed. It uses variance-weighted sampling based on past outcomes to focus evaluation on tasks near the agent's capability frontier, with the sampling distribution adapting as the harness evolves. It then estimates full-set scores from the sampled tasks by accounting for their sampling probabilities, enabling consistent comparisons across iterations despite evaluating different subsets. Experiments on online text classification and Terminal-Bench 2.1 show that Task-CoEvolve consistently outperforms fixed-subset baselines and matches the final performance of full-set search while reducing the number of evaluations during optimization by 80%. Code will be released at https://github.com/Agent4Science-UTokyo/Task-CoEvolve.

</details>


### [46] [Inject, Align, Recover: Staged Post-Training for Retrieval-Free Document Knowledge Internalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20281)
*Qian Kou,Xiaofeng Shi,Xiaosong Qiu,Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IAR（Inject-Align-Recover）三阶段后训练框架，将固定文档集转化为可用的参数化知识，用于免检索的问答任务。相比传统持续预训练，IAR通过注入、对齐和恢复三个阶段，在多个模型家族上显著提升领域内部化与通用能力之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在处理有界文档集合的问题时，若推理时不检索源文档，性能往往不佳。现有方法缺乏将固定语料库系统性地转化为参数化知识的系统性框架，因此需要一种能平衡领域内部化效果与通用能力保持的方法。

Method: IAR包含三个阶段：(1) Inject：将源文档转换为续写、重写和指令条件重建三种训练目标，实现结构化文档知识注入；(2) Align：使用仅含答案的QA监督对注入后的模型进行行为对齐；(3) Recover：将领域适配模型与基础指令模型合并，以恢复通用能力。

Result: 在Common Corpus（CC）和CCI数据集上，覆盖Llama、Phi、Qwen和SmolLM模型家族，IAR在8个数据集-模型组合中有7个在所有四项指标上优于Vanilla SFT，领域QA准确率平均提升3.6个百分点，IFEval、MMLU和MSBench上的平均通用性能提升12.1个百分点。扩展基线显示LoRA和FAPM可在个别通用指标上胜出，但在同时达到领先或接近领先的领域内部化效果的方法中，IAR保留了最强的通用性能图谱。

Conclusion: IAR通过解耦知识注入、行为对齐和通用能力恢复三个环节，有效提升了文档知识内部化的领域-通用能力前沿，展示了结构化注入目标与模型合并策略在免检索问答中的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Large language models often fail to answer questions about a bounded document collection when the source documents are not retrieved at inference time. We study this setting as document knowledge internalization: converting a fixed corpus into usable parametric knowledge for retrieval-free question answering. We propose IAR (Inject, Align, and Recover), a three-stage post-training framework that separates structured document knowledge injection, QA behavior alignment, and general ability recovery. Unlike conventional continued pretraining, Inject converts source documents into continuation, rewrite, and instruction-conditioned reconstruction objectives. Align then adapts the injected model with answer-only QA supervision, while Recover merges the domain-adapted model with the base instruction model to recover general capabilities. Across Common Corpus (CC) and CCI, and across Llama, Phi, Qwen, and SmolLM model families, IAR improves the domain-primary domain-general frontier for retrieval-free document internalization. In the main comparison, IAR improves over Vanilla SFT on all four reported metrics in 7 of 8 dataset-model settings, with average gains of 3.6 percentage points in domain QA accuracy and 12.1 percentage points in mean general performance across IFEval, MMLU, and MSBench. Extended CC baselines show that LoRA and FAPM can win individual general metrics, but among methods that also reach leading or near-leading domain internalization, IAR retains one of the strongest general profiles.

</details>


### [47] [Inducing Task Models from Computer-Use Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20319)
*Yucheng Jiang,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Ruishi Chen,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出任务模型归纳（TMI）方法，从自然主义计算机使用轨迹中自动发现潜在任务并归纳结构化任务模型，在受控实验中以0.974的一致性恢复交错任务，并重建74.9%的执行步骤，派生技能将保留任务准确率提升30.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体进入真实工作场景后，需要从被动记录的屏幕截图和鼠标键盘操作中学习任务的实际执行方式，组织也需要审计和复用这些知识。然而现有方法假设给定任务或单一工作流，且仅产生步骤级摘要而非结构化任务模型，无法应对真实工作中多线程、目标交错的复杂情况。

Method: TMI方法包含两个阶段：（i）在无约束轨迹中发现潜在任务，解开并发活动；（ii）对每个潜在任务归纳任务模型，该模型将递归目标分解的层次目标模型与组织执行的控制流程序模型配对。

Result: 在受控的人类和智能体轨迹上，TMI以0.974的一致性恢复交错任务分组，重建74.9%的可观测执行步骤，远超最强工作流归纳基线；由TMI任务模型派生的技能将保留任务准确率提升30.0%。

Conclusion: TMI能够从非结构化的自然主义使用轨迹中提取可审计、可复用的结构化任务模型，在任务发现、模型重建和下游技能提升方面均显著优于现有基线，为计算机使用智能体的工作学习与组织知识管理提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Naturalistic computer-use traces, passively recorded screenshots and mouse or keyboard actions, are a valuable resource for deriving symbolic, auditable, and reusable models of how everyday work is done. Such models matter as computer-use agents enter real work, where agents need to learn how tasks are actually performed, and organizations need to audit and reuse that knowledge. However, inducing such task models is challenging, as activity is observed only as low-level events and real-world work is multi-threaded with interleaved goals. Existing methods assume a given task or a single workflow, and produce step-level summaries rather than structured task models. We introduce Task Model Induction (TMI), which (i) discovers the latent tasks in an unconstrained trace, disentangling concurrent activity, and (ii) for each latent task, induces a task model pairing a hierarchical objective model of recursive goal decomposition with a procedure model of the control flow that organized the execution. Intrinsically, on controlled human and agent trajectories, TMI recovers interleaved tasks with 0.974 agreement against ground-truth groupings and reconstructs 74.9% of the observed execution steps, far more than the strongest workflow induction baseline. Extrinsically, skills derived from TMI's task models improve held-out task accuracy by 30.0% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [48] [G-CARL: Grounded Checklist-Aligned Reward Learning for Patient-Oriented Medical Report Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20331)
*Shiao Xie,Siyu Chen,Jianwei Lv,Bo Yuan,Yujin Wang,Xiandong Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出患者导向的医学报告解读（PMRI）这一新型开放多模态生成任务，旨在让模型根据用户查询和对话历史用准确且通俗的语言解释医学报告；并设计G-CARL框架（基于检索的原子声明验证+实例级加权检查清单的强化学习）以及真实基准MMedReport和三维评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者对医学报告的个人化解读需求日益增长，这既需要基于证据的医学事实准确性，又需要与患者情境相匹配的沟通方式。然而现有医学视觉语言任务未同时涵盖这两项要求，且这两者在可验证性上根本不同又紧密耦合，难以在传统监督微调和整体强化学习范式下联合优化。

Method: 提出G-CARL框架，一种基于grounded、checklist对齐的强化学习方法。它结合（1）多源检索用于原子声明（claim）的事实核验，和（2）上下文感知、实例级加权检查清单用于响应覆盖率监督，从而在不限制响应多样性的前提下，为事实性、用户需求满足度和表达质量提供结构化监督。同时构建了真实世界基准MMedReport及临床医生设计的三维评估协议。

Result: 大量实验表明G-CARL在整体质量、声明级精确率和检查清单召回率上均优于现有post-training基线；临床医生的成对偏好评估进一步确认G-CARL生成的解读更准确且更贴合患者需求。

Conclusion: G-CARL有效解决了医学报告解读中事实性与患者沟通双重目标的联合优化难题，验证了grounded、checklist对齐的强化学习在医疗多模态生成任务中的优越性，为该方向提供了新基准与评估范式。

Abstract: Personalized interpretation of medical reports has emerged as an increasingly important need among patients. Addressing this need requires both evidence-grounded medical factuality and context-dependent patient communication, yet existing medical vision-language tasks do not adequately capture these dual requirements. To bridge this gap, we introduce Patient-oriented Medical Report Interpretation (PMRI), a novel open-ended multimodal generation task that requires models to explain medical reports in accurate and accessible language based on a user's query and dialogue history. These two objectives differ fundamentally in their verifiability, yet remain tightly coupled, making them difficult to optimize jointly under conventional supervised fine-tuning and holistic reinforcement learning paradigms. To address this challenge, we propose G-CARL, a grounded, checklist-aligned reinforcement learning framework that combines multi-source retrieval for atomic claim verification with context-aware, instance-specific weighted checklists for response coverage, providing structured supervision for factuality, user-demand satisfaction, and expression quality without constraining response diversity. We further construct MMedReport, a real-world PMRI benchmark, along with a clinician-designed three-dimensional evaluation protocol. Extensive experiments demonstrate that G-CARL consistently outperforms existing post-training baselines in overall quality, claim-level precision, and checklist recall. Pairwise preference evaluation by clinicians further confirms that G-CARL produces interpretations that are more accurate and better aligned with patient needs.

</details>


### [49] [ConceptGuard: Benchmarking Context-Sensitive Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20338)
*Sahil Kale,Ian Harris*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"双重用途概念"(dual-use concepts)的概念，并构建了名为ConceptGuard的基准测试，用于在概念层面而非事实层面评估LLM的知识遗忘(unlearning)。现有遗忘方法在该基准上表现不佳，暴露出弱上下文分离、强遗忘-效用权衡等问题，为更符合现实安全需求的遗忘方法提供了方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM知识遗忘方法和基准仅基于互不相关的独立事实来构建遗忘集和保留集，并用简单直接的事实回忆来评估，无法完整评估遗忘能力。遗忘的关键需求是消除有害行为的同时保留良性知识，因此需要在概念层面进行完整的评估，而当前缺乏这样的基准。

Method: 作者引入"双重用途概念"这一概念，即既能用于有害场景又能用于良性场景的概念。基于此构建ConceptGuard基准，其中遗忘集和保留集在概念使用上是互补的，使遗忘评估在概念层面而非零散事实层面进行，评估是意图敏感的，旨在最大化上下文分离以促进更安全的行为。

Result: 实验表明现有遗忘技术在该基准下表现不佳，呈现弱的上下文分离以及差的ROUGE和概念级指标。结果揭示了强遗忘-效用权衡、上下文敏感性提升有限、以及不同方法在概念级控制上的一致性差等问题。

Conclusion: 当前遗忘技术无法满足现实世界的安全需求，需要开发在概念层面运作、能同时去除有害应用并保留良性使用的遗忘方法。该基准为未来更符合实际安全需求的遗忘研究提供了方向，数据集已公开。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly require selective removal of harmful or sensitive knowledge, called unlearning, yet existing methods and benchmarks fail to evaluate this capability completely. Current approaches rely on disjoint forget and retain sets composed of independent facts, and measure success using simple and direct factual recall. This framing fails to capture a key requirement of unlearning, namely the ability to eliminate harmful behaviors while preserving benign and beneficial knowledge. We argue that effective unlearning must operate at the level of concepts, ensuring complete removal of unsafe applications while maintaining their correct and useful usage, thereby achieving conceptually meaningful and complete unlearning. To better evaluate unlearning techniques from such a practical viewpoint, we introduce the notion of dual-use concepts: concepts that can be used in both harmful and benign contexts. Building on these concepts, we construct a benchmark called ConceptGuard where forget and retain sets are explicitly complementary in concept usage. Our benchmark uniquely enables unlearning to be explored and gauged at the level of concepts, instead of sparse facts, and evaluation is intent-sensitive with the goal of maximizing contextual separation to promote safer behavior. We demonstrate that current unlearning techniques perform poorly under this setting, showing weak contextual separation alongside poor performance in ROUGE and concept-level metrics. Our results reveal strong forgetting-utility trade-offs, limited gains in contextual sensitivity, and poor consistency in concept-level control across methods, and provide ideas for unlearning approaches that better align with real-world safety requirements. Our dataset is publicly available.

</details>

### [50] [Incident-Data Robustness Analysis of the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications (2026): How a Community-Expert Ranking Holds Up Against a Large-Scale LLM Incident Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19266)
*Kyriakos "Rock" Lambros,Steve Wilson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基于大规模真实事件语料库（7,714个快照，6,639个带标签事件）检验OWASP LLM应用十大风险排名与真实事件数据的一致性，发现专家排名与实际事件排名的Cohen's κ≈0.20，一致性较弱，且四个前沿分类器均未超越基于事件发生率基线的准确率0.863。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications是基于安全从业者社区专家投票的风险排名，但其是否与真实事件数据吻合尚未得到验证。作者提出一个更窄的问题：对照真实事件记录，专家排名是否与数据一致？

Method: 作者从CVE、GHSA、OSV和AIAAIC收集大规模LLM安全事件语料库（7,714个快照，6,639个按20类别 taxonomy 标注），使用贝叶斯测量误差模型校正分类器精度和召回率后推导出基于事件的排名。2026候选列表以固定权重（专家0.75，数据0.25）融合两种信号。通过预注册的前沿分类器"bake-off"竞赛和保留真值进行验证。

Result: 专家排名与事件数据排名一致性弱（Cohen's κ≈0.20，90%区间跨越零）；四个前沿分类器均未击败事件发生率基线的平衡准确率0.863；事件发生率基线排名与保留真值的Spearman ρ=0.918，保持稳健。

Conclusion: 专家排名本身是稳健的，但与真实事件数据的排名一致性较弱。尽管数据信号被纳入2026候选列表（权重0.25），其用于修正专家共识而非推翻它。作者强调这是探索性分析，不是OWASP官方发布，不取代官方流程。

Abstract: The OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications ranks the risks that a community of security practitioners judges most important. We ask a narrower question: checked against the record of real incidents, does that expert ranking agree with the data? We assembled a large-scale corpus of LLM-security incidents (7,714 snapshotted and 6,639 labeled against the 20-entry taxonomy) drawn from CVE, GHSA, OSV, and AIAAIC, and derived an incident-based ranking with a Bayesian measurement-error model that corrects each category's count for classifier precision and recall. The 2026 candidate list blends the two signals at fixed weights, 0.75 on the expert vote and 0.25 on the data, so the corpus corrects the consensus without overturning it. The agreement between the two rankings is weak: Cohen's $κ\approx 0.20$, with a 90% interval that crosses zero. The expert ranking is nonetheless robust. A pre-registered bake-off of four frontier classifiers returns no winner. None beats the incidence floor's balanced accuracy of 0.863. A ground-truth check leaves the floor's ordering (Spearman $ρ= 0.918$ against held-out truth) in place. This is an exploratory analysis by two working-group members, not the official OWASP release, and it does not supersede the official list or process.

</details>

*Hongmiao Yu,Silas Richelson,Jiachen Chen,K. K. Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ABEAT是一个基于属性基加密（ABE）的高效匿名动态群组通信系统，它通过隐藏密文中的属性来维持接收者匿名性，同时将非目标接收者的解密开销降低至现有技术的1/90以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态群组通信需要灵活的多人通信能力，但现有的基于ABE的群组通信方案缺乏接收者匿名性保护，这在紧急响应等应用场景中至关重要。此外，非目标接收者承担了不必要的解密开销，需要一种既能保持匿名性又高效的方案。

Method: ABEAT将属性基加密与基于图的命名空间相结合，提出了一种新的匿名KP-ABE方法。该方法在KP-ABE的密文中隐藏明文属性，防止多种试图破坏匿名性的攻击，并提供快速的接收者验证机制以降低非接收者的解密负担。

Result: ABEAT将非接收者的解密开销比现有最先进技术（如隐藏向量加密HVE）降低了90倍以上，甚至比不提供匿名性的FABEO方案还低40%。

Conclusion: ABEAT在保持接收者匿名性的同时实现了显著的性能提升，证明了匿名性与高效性可以在动态群组通信系统中同时实现。

Abstract: Confidential communication among a dynamic group of participants that ensures flexible and efficient many-to- many communication is highly desired capability. We leverage attribute-based encryption (ABE) for confidential group communication and enhance it by a graph-based namespace to create an efficient framework that allows groups to be formed and changed dynamically. In this paper, we focus on the important additional need to maintain the anonymity of recipients of a message, when using ABE for group communication for a variety of usage scenarios (e.g., emergency response).
  We propose ABEAT, an efficient and anonymous dynamic group communication system that also minimizes overhead on receivers who are not the intended recipients of a message. In ABEAT, we propose a new anonymous KP-ABE approach to maintain recipient anonymity. ABEAT hides the clear attribute in the ciphertext of KP-ABE, and also prevents several attacks that seek to break anonymity. ABEAT provides fast recipient verification, dramatically lowering the decryption overhead for non-recipients by more than a factor of 90 versus the current state of the art such as hidden vector encryption (HVE). In fact, it is even 40% less than FABEO, which offers no anonymity.

</details>

*Emanuel C. A. Valente,Lourenço A. P. Júnior,Leonardo Gonçalves Chahud,Júlio Cezar Estrella,Marcus Botacin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Aray，一个确定性优先的YARA解释器与正向夹具合成器。它能从YARA规则自动生成匹配该规则的良性测试文件（在不复现恶意行为的前提下），用于解决恶意软件样本难以分发导致的存储、CI、容灾演练和扫描器验证困难等问题。在416条公开规则上达到97.6%的整体成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: YARA规则易于分发，但用于证明正匹配的恶意软件样本本身难以分发，这使存储、持续集成、容灾演练以及可复现的扫描器验证变得复杂。而构造替代夹具（fixture）不仅要嵌入字面量，还需处理YARA条件中的备选、计数、偏移、整数读取和可执行容器约束，并且生成文件不能复现恶意行为。正验证是存在性的：只需要规则匹配集中的某一个文件成员，而非重建原始样本。

Method: Aray是一个确定性优先的YARA解释器和正向夹具合成器。模型可提出建设性的归一化或类型化提取回退方案，但绝不接触后端源码或二进制结构。由常规代码验证归一化后的规则、推导字符串和整数见证，并执行提取、路由、冲突检查布局以及ELF/PE/通用序列化。只有残余的归一化语义会被交给有界模型评判器。

Result: 在416条公开规则条目上评估：归一化阶段有182条无需模型协助即被接受，234条经模型归一化后被接受；可构造性预检接受406条，且每条被接受的夹具都与上游原始规则匹配。整体成功率406/416（97.6%），在可构造规则中为406/406（100%）。十个预期的预检处置，无扫描器不匹配或构造失败。不可达端点确认实现阶段零模型调用。两次锚定正则故障在最终运行前已修复，因此结果是修复后的系统结果，而非留出估计。

Conclusion: Aray证明了在几乎全部可构造规则上可以自动生成匹配且不含恶意行为的正向测试夹具，从而替代对真实恶意样本的分发依赖。原始规则预言机验证生成的夹具与源规则匹配；证明对所有可能文件的蕴含关系则是另一个更强的独立目标。

Abstract: A YARA rule is easy to distribute, but the malware sample used to demonstrate a positive match is not. This complicates storage, continuous integration, disaster-recovery exercises, and reproducible scanner validation. Constructing a replacement fixture requires more than embedding literals: YARA conditions can combine alternatives, counts, offsets, integer reads, and executable-container constraints, while the resulting file should not reproduce malware behavior. Positive validation is existential: it requires one file-level member of a rule's match set, not reconstruction of the originating sample. We present Aray, a deterministic-first YARA interpreter and positive-fixture synthesizer. Models may propose constructive normalizations or typed extraction fallbacks, but never backend source or binary structure. Conventional code validates normalized rules, derives string and integer witnesses, and performs extraction, routing, collision-checked layout, and ELF, PE, or generic serialization. Only residual normalization semantics reach a bounded model judge. We evaluated Aray over 416 public-rule entries. Normalization accepted 182 entries without model assistance and 234 after model normalization. Constructibility preflight admitted 406 entries, and every admitted fixture matched its upstream original rule. This yields 406/416 (97.6%) overall and 406/406 among constructible rules, with ten expected preflight dispositions and no scanner mismatches or construction failures. An unreachable endpoint confirmed zero model invocations during realization. The original-rule oracle validates generated fixtures against their source rules; proving implication for all possible files is a separate, stronger objective. Two anchored-regex failures were repaired before the final run, so these are post-fix systems results, not a held-out estimate.

</details>

Result: 成功开发了基于多元多项式方程求解难题（MQ问题）的可编辑认证数据结构构造工具，该构造具有后量子安全性。

Conclusion: 该工作为后量子时代可编辑认证数据结构的设计提供了新的方向，其安全性建立在多元多项式系统求解困难性的基础之上。

Abstract: We develop new tools for constructing redactable authenticated data structures with post-quantum security. In our construction, inverting the proposed one-way function means solving a polynomial equation (or a system of polynomial equations) in more than one variable. This is presently considered quantum-safe, i.e., there is no known quantum algorithm that could solve this problem efficiently if parameters are chosen wisely.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个实用的联邦学习框架，通过Tailscale安全组网和Flower框架实现跨地域协作的口腔癌筛查AI模型开发，评估了多种轻量级移动端架构，其中MobileNetV4-Conv-Small聚合的全局模型达到AUC 0.929、准确率87%的最佳性能。

Method: 构建实用的联邦学习框架：通过Tailscale实现客户端设备间安全组网和实时通信；使用Flower框架进行服务端聚合；客户端部署和编排手动配置，未使用企业级FL平台。评估了MobileNetV2、MobileNetV3Large、MobileNetV4-Conv-Small三种轻量级移动端架构，采用FedAvg算法进行全局模型聚合。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了一种无需企业级平台、基于开源工具即可实现的实用化联邦学习框架能够支持跨地域的口腔癌筛查AI协作开发，且轻量级模型在智能手机端可实现优秀的筛查性能。

Abstract: Data are the cornerstone of robust AI models. However, in the medical domain, access to reliable data is constrained by regulatory requirements and patient privacy, and clinical oral images are particularly difficult to obtain. Federated learning (FL) mitigates these constraints by enabling collaborative model development across decentralized datasets without centralizing or sharing patient data.
  This work presents a practical FL framework that supports geographically distributed collaboration among AI healthcare researchers and facilitates the development of robust models for oral cancer screening. Client devices were interconnected via Tailscale to provide secure networking and real-time communication. We implemented the FL workflow using the Flower framework for server-side aggregation, while client deployment and orchestration were configured manually; no enterprise FL platforms were used. To support a smartphone-based screening application, we evaluated lightweight, mobile-friendly architectures including MobileNetV2, MobileNetV3Large, and MobileNetV4-Conv-Small (MNv4-Conv-S). Across the global lightweight models aggregated using FedAvg, the MNv4-Conv-S based global model (GM-V4) achieved the best performance, reaching an AUC of 0.929 and an accuracy of 87%

</details>


### [55] [AEGIS: Attention-Embedding Gradient Isolation Shield - Triple-Channel Gradient Masking for Privacy-Preserving Federated LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19534)
*Ye Tao,Hong Shen,Hui Tian,Xin Wang,Can Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AEGIS是一种轻量级的联邦学习梯度反演防御方法，通过三个反向路径操作（冻结注意力投影参数、向嵌入梯度注入校准噪声、向MLP扩展梯度注入逐块噪声）同时关闭三种token信息泄露通道，在不改变架构的前提下将token恢复率降至接近零，同时保持或提升模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度反演攻击能从联邦学习中共享的梯度恢复私有训练文本，构成严重威胁。现有防御最多只针对一条泄露通道，要么降低模型效用，要么留下其余的结构性信号。作者通过分析transformer梯度结构，识别出三条独立的token信息泄露通道，需要设计一个能同时关闭所有三条通道的防御机制。

Method: 作者首先识别了三条信息泄露通道：通道1（注意力输出投影梯度中的低秩子空间编码输入嵌入）、通道2（嵌入梯度的行范数稀疏性直接揭示token存在性）、通道3（MLP扩展梯度携带类似通道1的可恢复子空间信号）。在此基础上提出AEGIS，包含三个仅作用于反向传播路径的操作：冻结注意力投影参数以结构性消除通道1；向嵌入梯度注入校准噪声以破坏通道2的token存在信号；向MLP扩展梯度注入逐块噪声以掩盖通道3。被掩蔽的梯度同时用于本地优化器和服务器导出，确保两侧均不保留干净信号。

Result: 在11个模型和6个数据集上的评估表明，AEGIS能将token恢复率降至接近零，对抗包括分析型和基于优化的各类梯度反演攻击均有效，同时保持或提升模型效用。作者还提供了通道1和通道2的形式化保证，并在自适应对抗者（完全了解防御机制）条件下进行了经验验证。

Conclusion: AEGIS通过系统性地识别并关闭所有三条梯度泄露通道，首次实现了对联邦学习中transformer梯度反演攻击的全面防御，无需架构改动且不牺牲模型效用，是联邦学习隐私保护的实质性进展。

Abstract: Gradient inversion attacks recover private training text from gradients shared in federated learning, posing a serious threat to collaborative model training. Through our analysis of transformer gradient structure, we identify three channels through which private token information leaks: the attention output projection gradient exposes a low-rank subspace that encodes input embeddings (Channel 1), the embedding gradient's row-norm sparsity directly reveals which tokens are present (Channel 2), and the MLP expansion gradient carries a recoverable subspace signal analogous to Channel 1 (Channel 3). State-of-the-art attacks exploit these channels analytically to achieve near-exact token recovery in seconds. Existing defences address at most one channel and either degrade model utility or leave the remaining structural signals intact.
  We introduce AEGIS (Attention-Embedding Gradient Isolation Shield), a lightweight defence that closes all three analytical channels with three backward-path operations requiring no architectural changes: freezing attention projection parameters eliminates Channel 1 by construction, calibrated noise injection into the embedding gradient destroys Channel 2's token-presence signal, and analogous per-block noise injection into the MLP expansion gradient masks Channel 3. The same masked gradient drives both the local optimiser step and the server export, so no clean signal is retained on either side.
  Evaluated across 11 models and six datasets, AEGIS reduces token recovery rates to near zero against a range of gradient inversion attacks, both analytical and optimisation-based, while preserving or improving model utility. We provide formal guarantees for Channels 1 and 2 and validate the full defence empirically against adaptive adversaries with complete knowledge of the mechanism.

</details>


### [56] [Enhancing Privacy in Federated Learning via Dual Obfuscation of Gradients and Training Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19650)
*Yuki Itabashi,Hiroto Sawada,Mare Hirose,Shoko Imaizumi,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双重混淆（dual obfuscation）方法，通过联合混淆更新信息和训练图像来增强联邦学习对图像重建攻击的鲁棒性。该方法结合基于随机二进制权重的梯度置零技术与图像加密技术，选择（ViT）上的实验表明，该方法在评估设置下减少了APRIL攻击恢复的视觉信息，且未造成超出图像加密本身的额外分类性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许客户端本地保留数据的同时进行协作训练，但已有研究表明共享的模型更新可以被用来重建训练数据。因此需要一种能够在梯度层面和图像层面同时提供防护的方法，以抵御图像恢复攻击。

Method: 提出双重混淆方法，包含两个互补组件：(1) 基于随机二进制权重的鲁棒性增强技术，将部分梯度元素随机置零，减少攻击者可获得的原始梯度信息；(2) 图像加密技术，降低重建图像的视觉可解释性。图像加密允许每个客户端和每张图像使用独立密钥，避免显式密钥共享。

Result: 在基于Vision Transformer（ViT）的图像分类任务上，该方法在评估设置下有效减少了APRIL攻击恢复的视觉信息量，并且不会造成除图像加密本身之外的额外分类性能下降。

Conclusion: 虽然该组合方法不提供绝对的安全保证，但实验结果表明，将梯度修改与图像加密相结合对于隐私增强联邦学习具有潜在价值。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training while keeping data locally at each client; however, recent studies have shown that training data can be reconstructed from shared model updates. To address this issue, this paper proposes a dual obfuscation method that enhances robustness against image restoration attacks by jointly obfuscating updated information and training images. The proposed method combines a robustness enhancement technique based on random binary weights, which randomly sets a portion of gradient elements to zero, with an image encryption technique. These techniques provide complementary protection by reducing the amount of original gradient information available to an attacker and the visual interpretability of reconstructed images, respectively. Furthermore, the image encryption technique allows independent keys to be used for each client and each image, avoiding explicit key sharing. Experimental results on an image classification task using a Vision Transformer (ViT) show that the proposed method reduces the visual information recovered by Attention Privacy Leakage (APRIL) under the evaluated settings without causing additional degradation in classification performance beyond that caused by image encryption. Although the proposed combination does not provide an absolute security guarantee, the results demonstrate the potential benefit of combining gradient modification and image encryption for privacy-enhanced federated learning.

</details>


### [57] [TGL-APT: Temporal Graph Learning with Graph Distillation for Efficient APT Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19750)
*Jing Chen,Ayong Ye,Yuanhuang Liu,Yuexin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TGL-APT是一个基于信息瓶颈节点自适应的APT攻击调查框架，通过图蒸馏、自适应时序图学习和跨时空攻击指纹对齐三个组件，在DARPA E3数据集上取得88.9%-95.7%的F1分数，同时相比KAIROS减少约39%训练时间、33%检测延迟和22%内存使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APT攻击具有隐蔽性和多阶段性，传统检测方法难以有效应对。虽然溯源图提供了丰富的行为上下文，但攻击相关证据稀疏且嵌在大量常规系统活动中，导致全图学习计算成本高且难以在长时间攻击序列间建立关联。

Method: TGL-APT包含三个互补组件：(1) 基于信息瓶颈的图蒸馏——抑制溯源冗余并约束结构失真，保持因果可达性；(2) 自适应时序图学习——随节点相关性演化持续精炼核心节点集；(3) 跨时空攻击指纹对齐——关联不同实体和时间窗口内碎片化的可疑活动。最后通过因果扩展和阶段表征重构完整的攻击过程。

Result: 在三个DARPA E3数据集上分别取得95.7%、90.9%和88.9%的F1分数；相比KAIROS，训练时间减少约39%、检测延迟减少约33%、内存使用减少约22%。

Conclusion: TGL-APT在检测性能、计算效率和调查能力之间取得了有效平衡，验证了基于信息瓶颈节点指导的自适应溯源图分析方法对APT检测的有效性。

Abstract: Advanced Persistent Threat (APT) attacks pose a critical challenge to modern systems, as their stealthy, multi-stage nature renders conventional detection methods ineffective. While provenance graphs provide rich behavioral context for attack investigation, attack-relevant evidence is often sparse and embedded in large volumes of routine system activity, making full-graph learning both computationally expensive and difficult to correlate over long attack sequences. We present TGL-APT, an adaptive investigation framework built on the observation that attack-relevant information is non-uniformly distributed and often mediated by structurally influential or behaviorally distinctive entities, which we characterize as information-bottleneck nodes. TGL-APT combines three complementary components: (1) information-bottleneck-guided graph distillation that suppresses provenance redundancy while bounding structural distortion and preserving causal reachability; (2) adaptive temporal graph learning that continuously refines the core node set as node relevance evolves; and (3) cross-spatiotemporal attack fingerprint alignment that associates fragmented suspicious activities across different entities and time windows. Finally, causal expansion and stage characterization reconstruct coherent attack processes for investigation. Experiments on three DARPA E3 datasets show F1-scores of 95.7%, 90.9%, and 88.9%, while reducing training time, detection latency, and memory usage by approximately 39%, 33%, and 22%, respectively, compared with KAIROS. These results demonstrate that TGL-APT effectively balances detection performance, computational efficiency, and investigation capability for provenance-based APT analysis.

</details>


### [58] [Survival of~the~Stealthiest: Evolving Low-Entropy Ransomware via~Genetic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19821)
*Efrat Levenberg,Kristina Sviazhina,Ayelet Butman,Pierre Parrend,Harel Berger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出将勒索软件执行建模为基于搜索的软件工程（SBSE）优化问题，利用遗传算法（GA）在系统活动统计偏差的硬约束下优化数据加密过程，以生成能规避行为监控系统检测的隐蔽勒索攻击模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统勒索软件的大规模加密操作会立即触发现代防御系统的检测，而现代威胁（即高级持续性威胁）倾向于在数小时甚至更长时间内保持隐蔽。现有研究缺乏对如何实现长时间隐匿执行的系统化方法，因此需要一种新的视角来生成隐蔽攻击模式。

Method: 将勒索软件攻击执行形式化为一个SBSE优化问题：以遗传算法（GA）为核心搜索技术，在"与基线系统活动的统计偏差"这一硬约束下，优化数据加密的执行策略，使攻击活动在统计上与正常系统行为不可区分，从而规避基于指纹识别的行为监控系统。

Result: 实验证明，经过进化（GA搜索）获得的攻击模式能够有效规避行为监控系统的指纹检测技术，验证了基于搜索的方法可以生成具备隐蔽性的勒索软件变体。

Conclusion: 基于搜索的方法为生成规避性恶意软件提供了强有力的框架，这对自动化软件防御系统提出了新的挑战，提示防御方需要应对搜索驱动的自适应攻击手段。

Abstract: Traditional ransomware deployment often relies on massive encryption procedure, triggering immediate detection by modern defense systems. This work introduces a paradigm shift in cryptographic attacks by framing ransomware execution as a Search-Based Software Engineering (SBSE) optimization problem. This approach addresses the persistence gap observed in modern threats, where attacks aim to remain undercover for hours rather than minutes. Using a Genetic Algorithm (GA), we optimize data encryption under a hard constraint on the statistical deviation from baseline system activity. We demonstrate that our evolved attack patterns can evade behavioral monitors under fingerprinting techniques. Our results suggest that search-based methods provide a powerful framework for generating evasive malware, highlighting an emerging challenge for automated software defense.

</details>


### [59] [MaliciousSkillBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Malicious Agent Skill Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19901)
*Yue Wang,Yi Liu,Gelei Deng,Ying Zhang,Yuekang Li,Zhenyu Chen,Leo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了MaliciousSkillBench，一个综合性恶意Agent Skill检测基准，整合13个公开来源、7,539个规范化唯一恶意身份和9,740个Skill样本，揭示了现有检测器在跨源评估下性能大幅下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skills通过可复用的指令包扩展LLM能力，但也创造了直接的恶意行为分发渠道。现有恶意Skill数据集分散在不同来源、格式和证据体系中，重复和结构相关的内容阻碍了直接聚合与评估，缺乏一个统一、全面的基准来验证恶意Skill检测方法的可靠性。

Method: 整合13个公开来源（其中11个贡献核心恶意工件），将8,414条原始恶意记录归约为7,539个规范化唯一身份、4,588个可操作结构族；经保守交叉标签冲突排除后构建含9,740个Skill（7,505恶意+2,235良性）的主基准；协调11个攻击类别对4,983个恶意身份进行标注；评估3个学习型文本检测器和3个现成Skill扫描器。

Result: 学习型检测器在随机划分下达到0.882-0.932 Random Macro-F1，但Source-Disjoint评估下仅0.653-0.665；最强word TF-IDF SVM在Random/structural-disjoint/Source-Disjoint上分别得分0.932/0.916/0.665，保留95.6%恶意召回但产生62.4%良性FPR；现成扫描器以牺牲恶意召回为代价降低误报。

Conclusion: 可靠恶意Skill检测需要更广泛的跨来源基准覆盖，以及同时衡量攻击检测与良性过度标记的联合评估方法；现有检测器在跨源泛化上表现不足，该基准为后续研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Agent Skills extend LLM agents with reusable instruction packages that may also include scripts, resources, and service configuration. This creates a direct distribution channel for malicious behavior, yet existing malicious-Skill datasets are fragmented across sources, artifact formats, evidence regimes, and benign coverage; duplicated and structurally related content further complicates direct aggregation and evaluation. We present MaliciousSkillBench, a comprehensive benchmark for malicious Agent Skill detection. We consolidate 13 public sources, 11 of which contribute Core malicious artifacts, and reduce 8,414 raw malicious records to 7,539 normalized-unique identities in 4,588 operational structural families. After conservative cross-label conflict exclusion, the primary benchmark contains 9,740 Skills: 7,505 malicious and 2,235 benign. To characterize its coverage, we harmonize 11 attack categories for 4,983 malicious identities with supported source-native mappings and find substantial differences in threat composition across sources. We then evaluate three learned text detectors and three off-the-shelf Skill scanners. Learned detectors achieve 0.882-0.932 Random Macro-F1 but only 0.653-0.665 under Source-Disjoint evaluation; the strongest word TF-IDF SVM scores 0.932/0.916/0.665 on Random/structural-disjoint/Source-Disjoint while retaining 95.6% malicious recall but producing 62.4% benign FPR on held-out sources. Off-the-shelf scanners occupy different but also unsatisfactory operating regimes, reducing false positives only at the cost of sharply lower malicious recall. Together, these results show that reliable malicious-Skill detection requires both broader cross-source benchmark coverage and evaluation that jointly measures attack detection and benign over-flagging.

</details>


### [60] [Securing Filesystems for Confidential Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19924)
*Dimitra Giantsidi,Antoine Delignat-Lavaud,Cédric Fournet,Jinnan Guo,Heidi Howard,Tianjiao Huang,Kapil Vaswani,Stavros Volos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ShieldFS是一个符合POSIX标准的文件系统，通过密码学承诺、哈希链和嵌入式Merkle树，在机密计算威胁模型下为持久存储提供端到端的完整性和新鲜性保证，无需修改应用程序。基于ZFS实现ShieldZFS，在保证安全性的同时性能与现有文件系统相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密计算虽然通过TEE保护了应用程序的计算过程，但存储仍然脆弱。即使使用磁盘加密，恶意的云提供商仍可对磁盘状态进行回滚、重放、分叉或篡改，破坏有状态应用所需的完整性和新鲜性保证。现有解决方案要么假设存储可信，要么开销过高，要么迫使应用程序集成完整性逻辑。

Method: ShieldFS在TEE内部维护简洁的密码学承诺来代表合法的文件系统状态，并在轻量级可信注册表中复制。磁盘上的数据结构（包括预写日志和存储池）通过哈希链和嵌入式Merkle树进行认证。系统利用事务和写时复制（CoW）技术原子性地更新持久文件系统状态和承诺。读取时验证承诺，确保即使整个I/O栈不可信也能检测回滚、重放和equivocation攻击。

Result: 通过扩展ZFS实现了ShieldZFS，使用标准文件系统基准测试和真实工作负载进行评估，结果显示ShieldZFS在提供强完整性和新鲜性保证的同时，性能与最先进的文件系统相当。

Conclusion: ShieldFS通过在TEE内维护密码学承诺并配合认证的磁盘数据结构，实现了无需应用修改、性能开销可控的端到端存储完整性保护，为机密计算环境下的持久存储安全提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Confidential computing protects applications inside Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs), but it leaves storage vulnerable. Even with disk encryption, a malicious cloud provider can roll back, replay, fork, or tamper with disk state, breaking the integrity and freshness guarantees required by stateful applications. Existing solutions either assume trusted storage, incur high overheads, or push integrity logic into applications. We present ShieldFS, a POSIX-compliant filesystem that provides end-to-end integrity and freshness for persistent storage in the confidential-computing threat model without requiring application changes. ShieldFS represents permissible filesystem states using succinct cryptographic commitments, maintained inside TEEs and replicated in a lightweight trusted registry. On-disk data structures, including a write-ahead log and a storage pool, are authenticated using hash chains and an embedded Merkle tree. ShieldFS utilizes transactions and copy-on-write to update persistent filesystem state and commitments atomically. The commitments are verified during reads, ensuring that rollback, replay, and equivocation attacks are detected even when the entire I/O stack is untrusted. We implement the design by extending ZFS, yielding ShieldZFS. Evaluation with standard filesystem benchmarks and real-world workloads shows that ShieldZFS provides strong integrity and freshness guarantees with performance comparable to state-of-the-art filesystems.

</details>


### [61] [ShadowPath: Lookup-Private Credential Status Verification over Authenticated State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19937)
*Patrick Herbke,Wolf Rieder,Christian René Sechting,Huaning Yang,Sid Lamichhane,Philip Raschke,Axel Küpper*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ShadowPath 是一种将凭据撤销状态检查转移到持有者端的方案，通过零知识证明向验证者证明凭据未被撤销，同时避免泄露可链接的元数据。论文首次评估了 Verkle 树在凭据撤销中的应用，并与稀疏 Merkle 树进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证凭据允许持有者在无需签发者参与的情况下出示数字签名声明，但撤销机制会引入隐私问题——现有状态检查可能暴露重复标识符、注册表位置或请求元数据，这些信息可被用作跨会话链接的稳定句柄。需要一种既支持撤销检查又不泄露可观察元数据的方法。

Method: ShadowPath 将凭据状态查找移至持有者端。每次出示时，持有者以零知识方式证明在验证者选择的注册表根下凭据未被撤销。论文将 Verkle 树与稀疏 Merkle 树进行对比评估，采用 Groth16 证明系统，测试验证 Verkle 树减少路径深度是否能抵消 KZG 认证的更高成本。实验涵盖 30 次桌面测试和两台主流移动设备的评估。

Result: 桌面测试中，稀疏 Merkle 树的 Groth16 证明中位耗时为 371.6ms，而 Verkle 树为 2.11s；验证分别耗时 3.70ms 和 7.55ms。在移动设备上，Verkle 树的 Groth16 证明耗时约 3 秒。结果表明更短的认证路径并不必然带来更廉价的零知识证明。在会话随机性独立假设下，验证者可见的状态数据不会泄露两次出示是否使用同一凭据，但该保证排除签发者-验证者共谋和同步流量场景。

Conclusion: ShadowPath 通过将撤销证明引入零知识证明领域，实现了持有者端的私有撤销检查。虽然 Verkle 树在理论上通过更短路径减少深度，但在 Groth16 中的实际证明成本反而高于稀疏 Merkle 树，表明树结构的选择需要综合考虑认证方案与证明系统的匹配度。该方案在会话值独立性假设下提供链接不可区分性保障，但存在共谋和同步流量等限制边界。

Abstract: Verifiable credentials let holders present digitally signed claims without requiring the issuer to participate in every presentation. Revocation complicates this privacy model because a verifier must determine whether a credential remains valid. Existing status checks may expose recurring identifiers, registry positions, or request metadata. Such information can serve as stable handles to link separate presentations. ShadowPath moves the credential status lookup to the holder. For each presentation, the holder proves, in zero-knowledge, that the credential has not been revoked under the verifier-selected registry root. The verifier learns the status result but not observable metadata. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first evaluation of Verkle trees for credential revocation and compare them with sparse Merkle trees to assess their applicability in real world applications. The comparison tests whether reducing path depth with Verkle trees offsets the higher cost of KZG-based authentication. Across 30 desktop trials, median Groth16 proving took 371.6ms with sparse Merkle and 2.11s with Verkle. Verification took 3.70ms and 7.55ms, respectively. Groth16 Verkle proving took about 3s on both primary mobile devices. The results show that shorter authenticated paths do not necessarily yield cheaper zero-knowledge proofs. With fresh session randomness, verifier-visible status data do not reveal whether two presentations use the same credential under the stated assumption of session-value independence. This guarantee excludes issuer-verifier collusion and synchronization traffic.

</details>


### [62] [From Noise to Signal: Improving Security Log Anomaly Detection Using LLMs with Endpoint-Specific Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19938)
*Christopher Henshaw,Gour Karmakar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于指令微调LLM的标准化分类框架，用于检测异常认证行为（包括边界案例）。在受控网络安全测试平台上，Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct取得了最佳端到端检测性能（准确率89.3%，召回率88.2%，F1值91.8%），显著优于Wazuh规则检测（准确率52.0%）和OpenSearch统计异常检测（准确率49.3%），尤其在边界场景检测中表现突出（80% vs 20% vs 15%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常日志检测方法存在明显局限：Wazuh等基于规则的检测依赖预定义规则，难以覆盖未知威胁；OpenSearch等统计方法只能识别与历史模式的偏差；LLM基础的方法则受限于提示构建、日志噪声以及缺乏端点特定认证行为的通用数据集。这些不足使得检测边界性异常认证行为尤为困难，亟需更鲁棒且具备语义理解能力的检测方案。

Method: 本文构建了一个受控网络安全测试平台以生成端点特定认证数据，产出包含正常、边界和异常三类行为场景的精选数据集。基于此数据集，开发了一个标准化指令化LLM分类框架，并对三个指令微调LLM（Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct、Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct、GPT-OSS 20B）进行评估，以Wazuh基于规则的检测和OpenSearch异常检测作为基线，使用统一的真实标签严重性框架进行比较。

Result: Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct表现最佳：准确率89.3%、召回率88.2%、F1值91.8%、假阴率11.8%，并检测到80%的边界异常场景。Wazuh准确率仅52.0%、假阴率68.6%、边界检测20%；OpenSearch准确率49.3%、假阴率74.5%、边界检测15%。Qwen检测性能较低但推理延迟最低且结构化响应有效性达100%。GPT-OSS在产生有效响应时分类性能较强。

Conclusion: 基于指令微调的LLM分类框架在异常认证行为检测上显著优于传统规则和统计方法，尤其在边界案例检测上优势明显。该研究表明LLM的语义理解能力可有效弥补传统检测方法的不足，为端点安全日志分析提供了新的方法论方向，同时不同LLM在性能、延迟和响应有效性上各有取舍，需根据实际部署需求权衡选择。

Abstract: Existing approaches to anomalous behaviour log detection, such as Wazuh rely primarily on predefined detection rules, while statistical anomaly detection approaches such as OpenSearch identify deviations from previously observed behavioural patterns. Recent research has investigated LLMs for log anomaly detection because of their ability to interpret semantic and contextual information. However, LLM-based approaches can be affected by prompt construction, noisy log data, and reliance on generic datasets that may lack endpoint-specific authentication behaviours. To address these limitations, this study develops a standardised instruction-based LLM classification framework for detecting anomalous authentication behaviours, including borderline cases. A controlled cybersecurity testbed was developed to generate endpoint-specific authentication data, producing a curated dataset comprising normal, borderline, and anomalous behavioural scenarios. Three instruction-tuned LLMs, Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct, Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct, and GPT-OSS 20B, were evaluated against Wazuh rule-based detection and OpenSearch Anomaly Detection using a common ground-truth severity framework. Meta Llama 3.1 8B Instruct achieved the strongest overall end-to-end detection performance, with an accuracy of 89.3%, recall of 88.2%, F1-score of 91.8%, and false negative rate of 11.8%. In comparison, Wazuh achieved an accuracy of 52.0% and false negative rate of 68.6%, while OpenSearch achieved an accuracy of 49.3% and false negative rate of 74.5%. Meta Llama also detected 80% of the borderline anomalous scenarios, compared with 20% for Wazuh and 15% for OpenSearch. Qwen achieved lower overall detection performance than Meta Llama but recorded the lowest average inference latency and 100% structured-response validity. GPT-OSS demonstrated strong classification performance when valid responses were produced.

</details>


### [63] [Tracking the Trend in How Speech Synthesizers Deceive People](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19959)
*Milan Šalko,Anton Firc,Kamil Malinka,Vojtěch Staněk,Martin Perešini,Filip Pleško,Jakub Reš*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究比较了IT专业人员对三种语音合成工具（发布于2019、2022、2024年）生成的深度伪造音频的检测能力，并对比了六种预训练检测器。结果显示，对于完全合成语音，F1分数从约90%（RTVC和YourTTS）降至48%（ElevenLabs）；对于部分合成（仅篡改一个句子），严格准确率降至9%，且人类与检测器以互补方式失效，均无法可靠定位短篡改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究报道人类对深度伪造语音的检测准确率为70-80%，但这些研究主要基于较旧的声音合成器。随着语音合成技术的快速进步，需要重新评估人类对现代和部分伪造语音的检测能力。

Method: 以82名IT专业人员为受试者，对三种分别于2019年、2022年和2024年发布的语音合成工具生成的音频进行人类检测实验，同时在相同材料上使用六种预训练检测器进行基准测试。实验涵盖完全合成（full spoofing）和部分合成（partial spoofing，仅篡改一个句子）两种条件。

Result: 完全合成条件下，F1分数从约90%（RTVC和YourTTS）降至48%（ElevenLabs），即使明确警告存在深度伪造。部分合成条件下，严格准确率降至9%，受试者77%的时间将合成句子误判为真实。人类和检测器以互补方式失效，均不能可靠定位短篡改。此外，受试者越来越倾向于将真实语音误标为伪造，侵蚀了对未操作音频的信任。

Conclusion: 人类感知对现代和部分伪造条件下的检测不可靠，这推动了程序化验证、来源追溯、水印和段级检测等技术的需求。

Abstract: Advances in speech synthesis have made deepfake audio highly realistic. Earlier studies reported 70-80% human detection accuracy, but relied primarily on older synthesizers. We compare human detection for three selected voice synthesis tools released in 2019, 2022, and 2024 with 82 IT professionals, and benchmark humans against six pretrained detectors on the same material. For fully synthetic speech (full spoofs), the F1 score drops from about 90% for RTVC and YourTTS to 48% for ElevenLabs, although listeners were explicitly warned that deepfakes were present. For partial spoofing, where only one sentence of an utterance is altered, strict accuracy falls to 9%, and listeners classify the synthetic sentence as bona fide 77% of the time. Humans and detectors fail in complementary ways, and neither reliably localizes short manipulations. Additionally, listeners increasingly mislabel bona fide speech as fake, eroding trust in unmanipulated audio. These findings show that human perception alone is unreliable for the selected modern and partial-spoof conditions and motivate procedural verification, provenance, watermarking, and segment-level detection.

</details>


### [64] [COPA: Continual Preference Optimization for Adaptive Prompt Injection Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19982)
*Roshan Sood,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: COPA是一个持续偏好优化框架，将提示注入防御视为终身学习问题，通过GRPO优化和基于边际权重的经验回放机制，实现对不断演化的对抗性攻击的持续适应，同时缓解灾难性遗忘并保持通用能力，在持续攻击流上将攻击成功率降低了最高6.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM提示注入防御主要依赖静态方法，如固定对齐目标或攻击特定过滤机制，需要在新攻击策略出现时重新设计。虽然近期终身对齐方法能处理用户偏好漂移，但无法应对持续演化的对抗性攻击者，这种局限在实际部署中尤为关键，因为攻击分布的演化要求持续适应且不能牺牲对已有威胁的鲁棒性。

Method: COPA框架将提示注入防御建模为终身学习问题，采用两个核心机制：1）基于GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）的增量优化，不断从新观察到的攻击中纳入反馈信号；2）margin-weighted经验回放机制，通过加权重放历史对抗样本以保留对先前攻击类别的防御能力，从而缓解灾难性遗忘并维持模型通用能力。

Result: 在终身提示注入攻击流上的实验中，COPA相比最先进的防御方法，将攻击成功率平均降低了4.4倍，最高降低6.3倍，同时保持了通用能力的稳定。

Conclusion: 持续偏好优化被证明是防御LLM面对自适应对手的有效范式，为动态对抗环境下的LLM安全对齐提供了一种可持续迭代而非一次性修复的解决方案。

Abstract: LLMs remain vulnerable to prompt injection attacks, where adversarial instructions embedded in user inputs or external content manipulate model behavior and bypass safeguards. Existing defenses are predominantly static, relying on fixed alignment objectives or attack-specific filtering mechanisms that require redesign as new attack strategies emerge. While recent lifelong alignment methods address shifting user preferences, they do not account for adaptive adversaries that continually evolve to exploit weaknesses in previously learned defenses. This limitation is particularly important in real-world deployments, where evolving attack distributions necessitate continual adaptation without sacrificing robustness to previously encountered threats. We present COPA, a continual preference optimization framework that treats prompt-injection defense as a lifelong learning problem. Instead of one-time alignment, COPA incrementally incorporates feedback from newly observed attacks via GRPO-based optimization and uses margin-weighted experience replay to retain defenses against prior attack classes. This enables continuous adaptation to emerging threats while mitigating catastrophic forgetting and preserving general-purpose model capabilities. Across lifelong prompt injection attack streams, COPA reduces attack success rate by up to 6.3x and 4.4x on average compared to state-of-the-art defenses. These results highlight continual preference optimization as an effective paradigm for defending LLMs against adaptive adversaries.

</details>


### [65] [EchoCoT: Extracting Hidden Chain-of-Thought from Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20055)
*Yiting Qu,Ziqing Yang,Chi Cui,Ye Leng,Junjie Chu,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出EchoCoT攻击方法，通过在工具调用之间利用"推理重放表面"，从黑盒大规模推理模型（LRM）中近乎逐字提取隐藏的思维链（CoT），实验表明在开源与专有模型上均可实现高成功率的CoT提取，揭示了隐藏CoT提取是一个实际的安全威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿专有大语言推理模型（LRM）中的隐藏思维链（CoT）是极具价值的模型资产，但目前对于能否通过黑盒API交互直接提取这些隐藏CoT的研究几乎空白。作者希望系统性探究这一安全漏洞，以引起对隐藏CoT资产保护的重视。

Method: 作者识别出工具调用之间一个此前被忽视的"推理重放表面"（reasoning replay surface），开发了多步攻击方法EchoCoT，利用API返回的保真信号迭代提取隐藏CoT；同时构建了一种基于LLM的优化框架，自动搜索跨数据集有效的通用注入轨迹。评估覆盖3个开源LRM和5个前沿专有LRM。

Result: 在开源LRM上，EchoCoT达到最高66.4%的近乎逐字提取成功率，提取轨迹长度与目标相差不超过10%，且至少90%的token与目标CoT完全匹配；相同的注入轨迹可泛化到未见数据集，最高达80%提取成功率。在专有LRM上，大量提取的CoT与提供商报告的长度和可用的CoT摘要高度吻合；在Gemini-2.5上成功提取了33,463个token（目标为32,948个token）的超长CoT。

Conclusion: 隐藏CoT提取已被证明是一个切实可行的安全风险，当前黑盒LRM的防护机制不足以保护其隐藏推理过程，需要更好的隐藏CoT资保护策略。

Abstract: Hidden chain-of-thought (CoT) traces, especially those from frontier proprietary large reasoning models (LRMs), are valuable model assets. Yet whether these hidden CoTs can be directly extracted from black-box models remains largely unexplored. In this work, we systematically study whether hidden CoTs can be extracted near-verbatim from black-box LRMs through API interactions. We identify a previously overlooked reasoning replay surface between tool calls and develop EchoCoT, a multi-step attack that iteratively extracts hidden CoTs using API-returned fidelity signals. We further develop an LLM-based optimization framework that automatically searches for an effective universal injection trajectory across various datasets. We evaluate EchoCoT on three open-source and five frontier proprietary LRMs. On open-source LRMs, EchoCoT achieves up to 66.4\% near-verbatim extraction success, with the extracted trace length within 10\% of the target and at least 90\% of tokens exactly matching the target CoT. The same injection trajectory also generalizes to unseen datasets, achieving up to 80\% extraction success under the same criterion. For tested frontier proprietary LRMs, a substantial fraction of extracted CoTs closely align with provider-reported reasoning lengths and available CoT summaries. EchoCoT can also extract very long CoTs: on Gemini-2.5, it extracts 33,463 tokens from a 32,948-token target. These results establish hidden-CoT extraction as a practical security risk and highlight the need to better protect hidden CoT assets.

</details>


### [66] [TrustRAG: Blockchain-Enhanced RAG via Committee-Based Credibility Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20097)
*Baixiang Liu,Haotian Che,Yuan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrustRAG是一个基于委员会和区块链的检索增强生成系统，通过零知识协议、安全多方计算和跨链哈希承诺来保证文档可信度与完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统依赖集中式数据库，缺乏文档来源验证、防篡改和信任评估机制，在医疗、金融、物流、法律等高要求领域，错误或被篡改的文档可能导致严重决策失误。

Method: TrustRAG采用三项核心技术：(1) 领域专家委员会通过零知识协议对文档进行认证；(2) 利用安全多方计算将委员会的隐藏评分合成为可验证的信任分数；(3) 通过跨链哈希承诺联合维护文档数据和信任分数，防止任何静默修改或删除。

Result: 该框架实现了文档和评分的不可篡改性，每个排名都可以独立重放和验证，客户端能够验证任何信任分数及文档来源。

Conclusion: TrustRAG通过委员会认证与区块链技术为RAG系统提供了可信、可验证的文档来源保障，解决了集中式数据库在来源验证、防篡改和信任评估方面的核心缺陷。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) lets Large Language Models (LLMs) pull in up-to-date, domain-specific information instead of relying only on what they were trained on. Yet most RAG systems still draw from centralized databases with limited oversight, making it difficult to verify where a document came from, whether it has been tampered with, or whether it should be trusted at all. This is a serious problem in domains where both the timeliness and accuracy of retrieved content are critical, such as healthcare, finance, logistics, and legal case law, where a wrong or manipulated document can directly lead to bad decisions.
  We present TrustRAG, a committee-based, blockchain-backed RAG system: before a document is used, it is certified by a committee of domain experts through a zero-knowledge protocol, and the committee's hidden scores are combined via secure multi-party computation into a trust score that any client can verify. These scores, along with the underlying document data, are maintained jointly across chains through hash commitments, so no document or score can be silently altered or dropped, and every ranking can be independently replayed and checked.

</details>


### [67] [A Meta-Study on Replication Papers in Usable Security & Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20108)
*Christian Mack,Benjamin Berens,Hanna Algedri,Tobias Hilt,Daniela Reimer,Peter Mayer,Melanie Volkamer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文采用混合方法系统性研究了可用安全与隐私领域中复制研究（replication studies）的现状。通过分析13个会议/期刊的征稿要求、系统检索2016-2025年间24篇复制用户研究论文、运用Olszewski等人（2025）的复制分类法进行归类，并对作者进行问卷调查，揭示了四个关键洞见：征稿指南需更清晰、复制论文中修改细节难以追溯、严格精确复制不存在（约三分之二的研究修改了多个方面）、时间与情境变化是复制的主要动机。文章最后为会议、研究者和审稿人提供了加强复制实践的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可用安全与隐私领域是一个年轻且不断扩展的研究领域，其研究标准（特别是关于复制研究的标准）仍在发展中。作者希望全面了解该领域当前复制研究的现状，以帮助建立更成熟的研究实践标准。

Method: 采用混合方法：（1）审查13个安全/隐私/人机交互场所的征稿要求；（2）在2016-2025年间对这些场所发表的复制用户研究论文进行系统搜索，得到24篇；（3）使用Olszewski等人（2025）提出的复制分类法对这24篇论文进行分类；（4）向这些论文的作者分发调查问卷以了解其进行复制研究的动机。

Result: 分析揭示了四项关键发现：（A）征稿启事需要为作者和审稿人提供更清晰的复制研究指南；（B）阅读复制论文时很难确定对原始研究做了哪些修改；（C）样本中不存在严格的精确复制，约三分之二的研究修改了原始工作的多个方面；（D）影响结果的时间与情境变化是复制研究最常见的动机之一。

Conclusion: 基于研究结果，作者为会议组织者、研究者和同行评审人员提供了切实可行的建议，以加强可用安全与隐私领域的复制实践，推动该领域研究标准的成熟。

Abstract: The field of usable security and privacy research is a young and expanding field, which is still developing standards for its research, e.g. regarding replications. We used a mixed-method approach, in order to get a better understanding of the current state of replications in the field of usable security and privacy: (1) we examine the Call for Papers of 13 venues spanning security, privacy, and human-computer interaction; (2) we conduct a systematic search for papers reporting replicated user studies published across these venues between 2016 and 2025, yielding 24 relevant publications; (3) we categorized these 24 papers employing the replication taxonomy proposed by Olszewski et al. (2025); (4) we distributed a survey to the authors of these papers to understand their motivations for conducting replications. Our analysis reveals four key insights: (A) Calls for Papers would benefit from clearer guidelines for authors and reviewers regarding replication work; (B) determining what modifications were made relative to the original study proves difficult when reading replication papers; (C) strict exact replications do not exist in our sample. Approximately two-thirds of the 24 studies altered multiple aspects of the original work; (D) temporal and contextual changes affecting results emerged as one of the most frequently cited motivations for replication. Based on these findings, we offer practical recommendations for venues, researchers, and peer reviewers to strengthen replication practices in usable security and privacy research.

</details>


### [68] [Privacy-Preserving Detection of Rare Disease-Associated Cell Subsets via Secure Multi-Party Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20118)
*Ş. Selcan Magara,Esther Havemann,Debora Jutz,Ali Burak Ünal,Mete Akgün*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于安全多方计算(MPC)的框架，使得CellCnn卷积神经网络能够完全在秘密共享数据上进行训练和推理，在不暴露原始患者数据的前提下实现稀有疾病相关细胞亚群的准确检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有疾病相关细胞亚群的检测对理解白血病和病毒感染至关重要，但训练可靠的CellCnn模型需要大规模、多样化的患者队列，单个机构难以满足。同时，单细胞数据的敏感性使得跨机构共享受到隐私法规限制，需要一种能在加密数据上训练和推理的解决方案。

Method: 提出一个基于安全多方计算(MPC)的框架，使CellCnn的完整训练和推理过程全部在秘密共享数据上运行，确保参与方和计算服务器都无法看到原始患者数据或中间值。与以往移除ReLU激活和偏置项的隐私保护方法不同，本方法保留了CellCnn架构中的所有关键组件。

Result: 在巨细胞病毒感染(CMV)和急性髓系白血病(AML)的基准单细胞数据集上，该方法保持了与明文版本接近的准确性，且优于之前的隐私保护基线方法。

Conclusion: 该MPC框架在不暴露原始患者数据的前提下，能够保留CellCnn完整架构（包括ReLU激活和偏置项）并实现准确的稀有细胞亚群检测，为跨机构协作的隐私保护单细胞分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The detection of rare disease-associated cell subsets from high-dimensional single-cell measurements is critical for understanding diseases such as leukaemia and viral infections. CellCnn, a convolutional neural network (CNN) designed for this task, has demonstrated the ability to identify phenotype-associated cell populations at frequencies as low as 0.01\%. Training such models reliably requires patient cohorts that are larger and more diverse than any single institution can typically assemble, and the underlying single-cell data is too sensitive to share across institutional boundaries under existing privacy regulations. We propose a secure multi-party computation (MPC) framework that enables the training and inference of CellCnn entirely on secret-shared data. This ensures that neither the participants nor the computing servers ever observe raw patient data or intermediate values. Evaluated on benchmark single-cell datasets for cytomegalovirus infection (CMV) and acute myeloid leukaemia (AML), our implementation preserves accuracy close to its plaintext counterpart while outperforming the prior privacy-preserving baseline. In contrast to earlier privacy-preserving approaches that removed components such as ReLU activations and bias terms, our method retains these key parts of the CellCnn architecture and supports accurate analysis without exposing raw patient data.

</details>


### [69] [Trustworthy mobile edge caching: a blockchain approach to mitigate malicious nodes and incentivize cache sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20145)
*Motahare Ebrahimi,Nastooh Taheri Javan,Seyedakbar Mostafavi,Fatemeh Pakzaban*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于区块链的信任管理机制，用于移动边缘网络中缓存服务的可信节点识别与激励，通过智能合约计算直接和间接信任值，并引入奖励机制和认证机制分别应对节点自私行为和身份伪造问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动网络流量持续增长，边缘服务器缓存对于降低延迟至关重要，但面临三个关键挑战：恶意边缘服务器可能删除或篡改缓存内容、边缘服务器容量有限需要移动helper节点贡献缓存资源、helper节点因自私行为需要激励机制且也可能存在恶意行为。

Method: 提出基于区块链的信任管理机制，通过智能合约计算直接信任和间接信任来准确识别可信的边缘服务器和移动节点；基于移动节点对服务质量（QoS）的满意度确定可信度，将信任数据安全存储在区块链上；引入奖励机制激励缓存共享，对抗节点自私性；同时设计基于区块链的认证机制以防御节点仿冒攻击，并在共识过程中考虑节点移动性、能耗和计算能力约束，优化信任、缓存容量和成本效率。

Result: 仿真结果表明，即使在数据中存在10%的噪声的情况下，该方法仍能准确区分诚实节点和恶意服务器。

Conclusion: 该区块链信任管理机制能有效解决移动边缘缓存中的恶意节点识别、自私行为激励和身份伪造等挑战，在存在噪声干扰时仍保持较高的信任判断准确性。

Abstract: As mobile network traffic continues to grow, content caching on edge servers is critical for reducing latency. However, challenges such as malicious edge servers that may delete or manipulate cached content, along with the limited capacity of these servers, need to be addressed. To overcome the capacity limitations, helper mobile nodes can contribute their cache resources. However, due to their selfish behavior, an incentive mechanism is necessary to encourage resource sharing. Additionally, these helper nodes can also be malicious. This paper proposes a blockchain-based trust management mechanism that addresses these challenges by accurately identifying trustworthy edge servers and mobile nodes. The proposed mechanism calculates both direct and indirect trust using smart contracts, ensuring that malicious nodes are effectively filtered out. Trustworthiness is determined based on mobile node satisfaction with the quality of service, and trust data is securely stored on the blockchain. To combat node selfishness, a reward mechanism is introduced to incentivize cache sharing. Furthermore, a blockchain-based authentication mechanism protects against node impersonation. Our approach optimizes trust, cache capacity, and cost efficiency while considering mobile node mobility, energy consumption, and computational power constraints during the consensus process. Simulation results show that the proposed method can accurately distinguish between honest and malicious servers, even with a 10% noise in data.

</details>


### [70] [Chameleon: Robust Defense Against Tor Website Fingerprinting via Many-to-Many Traffic Morphing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20160)
*Yuwen Cui,Kai Wei,Kehan Shen,Ning Wang,Zhuo Lu,Yao Liu,Guangjing Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Chameleon，一种基于多对多随机流量变形（many-to-many randomized traffic morphing）的鲁棒网站指纹（WF）防御方法。它通过选择类内多样性和类间差异小的变形候选，并允许不同网页共享变形目标来增加攻击者的不确定性。Chameleon引入了基于基数树（radix-trie）的同步机制以支持Tor可插拔传输（PT）部署。实验表明，与Adaptive Tamaraw相比，Chameleon在降低对抗训练攻击精度上最高达36.74%，同时显著降低带宽和时间开销，并在DAAE攻击和真实PT桥接部署中均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WF防御大多存在可学习的网页流量映射特征，且针对对抗训练鲁棒的防御并不一定抵抗基于防御感知自编码器（DAAE）的攻击，需要一种更强的防御方案。

Method: Chameleon采用多对多随机流量变形策略：选择高类内多样性和低类间差异的变形候选，将每个网页流量随机映射到多个候选目标，并允许不同网页共享变形目标以增加对抗不确定性。为部署于Tor，引入基数树同步机制，利用包方向前缀使PT端点识别一致的变形流量，并结合流量变异和归一化前缀匹配降低开销。

Result: 在三个公开数据集、六种先进防御和五种WF攻击的闭集及开放集测试中：相比Adaptive Tamaraw，对抗训练攻击精度降低最高36.74%，带宽和时间开销分别降低34.12%和60.38%；在GTT23上对DAAE攻击，Chameleon将F1分数限制在35.19%（Adaptive Tamaraw仅限制到88.22%）；真实PT桥接部署中仅引入16.25%时间开销。

Conclusion: Chameleon通过多对多随机变形有效提升了WF防御的鲁棒性，既能抵抗对抗训练攻击也能抵抗DAAE攻击，且在Tor真实部署场景中开销可控，优于现有防御方案。

Abstract: Website fingerprinting (WF) attacks can infer users' browsing activities from encrypted Tor traffic by exploiting side-channel features. Although many WF defenses have been proposed, we find that most existing defenses create learnable web trace mapping features. We further show that robustness against adversarial training does not necessarily imply robustness against defense-aware autoencoder (DAAE)-based attacks.
  To address these limitations, we present Chameleon, a robust WF defense based on many-to-many randomized traffic morphing. Chameleon selects morphing candidates with high intra-class diversity and low inter-class disparity. Chameleon randomly maps each webpage trace to multiple candidates, and allows different webpages to share morphing targets, thereby increasing adversarial uncertainty. For practical Tor deployment, Chameleon introduces a radix-trie-based synchronization mechanism that enables pluggable transport (PT) endpoints to identify consistent morphing traces using packet-direction prefixes, together with trace mutation and normalized prefix matching to reduce overhead. We evaluate Chameleon against six state-of-the-art defenses and five WF attacks on three public datasets in closed- and open-world settings. Compared with Adaptive Tamaraw, Chameleon reduces adversarial-training-based attack accuracy by up to 36.74% while reducing bandwidth and time overhead by 34.12% and 60.38%, respectively. Under DAAE-based RF attacks on GTT23, Chameleon limits attack performance to 35.19% F1-score while Adaptive Tamaraw only limits it to 88.22% F1-score. In the real-world PT bridge evaluation, Chameleon substantially reduces the effectiveness of strong WF attacks while incurring only 16.25% time overhead.

</details>

### [71] [Active Inference as Context Acquisition for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19202)
*Sanchayan Dutta,Sai Niranjan Ramachandran,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将交互式AI智能体的上下文获取问题形式化为主动推理（active inference）框架：内层推理更新对潜在任务状态的信念，外层决策选择下一个上下文动作、任务动作或停止动作，以最小化带代价的期望自由能。在确定性设置中，认知项简化为期望信息增益（可除以token代价进行归一化），并实例化为OQA（Optimal Question Asking）框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式AI智能体需要尽可能高效地获取正确的上下文。当用户省略约束、偏好、文件或任务变量时，智能体可以选择默认假设或花费token进行澄清提问、检索调用、工具调用或提示尝试——这一权衡缺乏统一的数学形式化。

Method: 提出主动推理框架用于上下文获取：内部推理步骤更新对潜在任务状态的信念，外部决策步骤在上下文动作、任务动作和停止动作之间选择以最小化期望自由能（含代价）。在确定性设置下认知项退化为期望信息增益（可按token成本归一化）。框架实例化为OQA（最优提问），采用精确后验和动态规划oracle。

Result: 在25到300个候选的二元和多分类任务上对前沿语言模型进行基准测试；还研究了生成前澄清和token预算下的自动提示优化。

Conclusion: 该框架是模型无关的，将主动推理视为AI智能体上下文获取层的设计原则。

Abstract: Interactive AI agents must acquire the right context as efficiently as possible. When a user omits a constraint, preference, file, or task variable, an agent can proceed with a default assumption or spend tokens on a clarifying question, retrieval call, tool call, or prompt trial. We formulate this tradeoff as active inference for context acquisition. An inner inference step updates beliefs over a latent task state, and an outer decision selects the next context action, task action, or stop action to minimize expected free energy under cost. In deterministic settings, the epistemic term reduces to expected information gain, optionally normalized by token cost. We instantiate the framework in Optimal Question Asking (OQA), with exact posteriors and a dynamic programming oracle, and benchmark frontier language models on binary and multiway categorical tasks from 25 to 300 candidates. We also study clarification before generation and automated prompt optimization under token budgets. The formulation is model-agnostic and views active inference as a design principle for the context-acquisition layer of AI agents.

</details>


### [72] [Robust Metaheuristics under Uncertainty for Berth Allocation and Quay Crane Assignment: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19214)
*Yang Li,Peilan Xu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统地综述了不确定环境下用于泊位分配与岸桥调度问题（BACAP）的鲁棒群体元启发式优化方法，从机制视角组织现有方法并提出了基准测试套件与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
